{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"blanches","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"blanching","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"blanched","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"blanched","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"His cheek blanched with fear.","type":"example"},{"text":"The rose blanches in the sun.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To grow or become white."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to blanch linen","type":"example"},{"text":"Age has blanched his hair.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To take the color out of, and make white; to bleach."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cook by dipping briefly into boiling water, then directly into cold water."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["cooking","food","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["To whiten, for example the surface of meat, by plunging into boiling water and afterwards into cold, so as to harden the surface and retain the juices."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To bleach by excluding light, for example the stalks or leaves of plants by earthing them up or tying them together."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to blanch almonds","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make white by removing the skin of, for example by scalding."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To give a white lustre to (silver, before stamping, in the process of coining)"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cover (sheet iron) with a coating of tin."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To give a favorable appearance to; to whitewash; to whiten;"],"synonyms":[{"word":"palliate"}],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/blɑːnt͡ʃ/"},{"ipa":"/blænt͡ʃ/"}],"upstream_line":235177,"upstream_offset":1152058340,"upstream_record_sha256":"960160fac43de4e392fb1f405a48f523d2170df4d627d0709592b790eef8bfdf","word":"blanch"}

a tertial."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences","ornithology"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A gigaton-scale weapon would of necessity be a three-stage design (incorporating a fusion tertiary - likely with its own massive fission tamper - in addition to the fission primary and fission-jacketed fusion secondary), as a reasonably-sized primary would likely have difficulty imploding to ignition the enormous secondary that would be required to reach a one-gigaton yield with a two-stage thermonuclear.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A large stage in some extremely powerful thermonuclear weapons (resembling a greatly-enlarged secondary) which is compressed by the explosion of the secondary until ignition of nuclear fusion takes place, in much the same manner as the secondary is imploded by the primary, and which can allow for the attainment of yields of many tens or even hundreds of megatons, and likely even greater;
